## Key

<table><tr><td>Article Title</td><td>Long-term recovery of Mediterranean ant and bee communities after fire in southern Spain</td></tr><tr><td>Authors</td><td>J. Manuel Vidal-Cordero, Elena Angulo, Francisco P. Molina, Raphaël Boulay and Xim Cerdá</td></tr><tr><td>Corresponding author</td><td>xim@ebd.csic.es</td></tr></table>

<table><tr><td>File Dataset_S1</td><td>Contains the data analysed in this article</td></tr></table>

<table><tr><td>Spreadsheets</td><td>data.ants: contains the values of the taxonomic indices, functional indices, and functional traits for each ant sampling transect as well as the values for the associated environmental variables</td></tr><tr><td></td><td>data.bees: contains the values of the taxonomic indices, functional indices, and functional traits for each bee sampling transect as well as the values for the associated environmental variables</td></tr><tr><td></td><td>data.veg: contains the data on the vegetation strata for each transect</td></tr></table>

<table><tr><td>ant.matrix: contains the data used to calculate the taxonomic indices</td></tr><tr><td>bee.matrix: contains the data used to calculate the taxonomic indices</td></tr><tr><td>ant.traits: contains the full list of traits for each species, along with references and detailed descriptions</td></tr><tr><td>bee.traits: contains the full list of traits for each species, along with references and detailed descriptions</td></tr><tr><td>references: contains the main references for the ant and bee traits</td></tr></table>
